==============================================================================
De arte cabalistica libri tres
Johannes Reuchlin (1455-1522) (1517)
Edition staged: Hagenau: Thomas Anshelm, March 1517 (first edition). Wellcome Library scan via Internet Archive (b13135004_0001/_0002), both parts combined.
Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/b13135004_0001
Rights: Public Domain. Author died 1522; work published 1517, well before 1930. The modern English translation (Goodman, Univ. of Nebraska Press, 1993) is in copyright.
Language: Latin (with Hebrew and Greek)
Staged for the Tower of Babel digital library (antarctic-labs.com).
The founding text of Christian Kabbalah: dialogue between Simon the Kabbalist, Marranus the Muslim, and Philolaus the Pythagorean. Companion: De verbo mirifico (1494), not staged here. OCR of early-modern print with Hebrew; expect noise.
